         E-payment Terminals.                                                                                                        付。

                                                                                                                                     構。

Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（ 僅供參考）


ANDROID SMART E-PAYMENT SOLUTION (A-SERIES)
智能電子支付解決方案（A系列）

General features:    Powered by Android operating system, PCI PTS 5.x certified, the latest certification in the industry.
                     Compact, portable, equipped with a large high resolution touch screen and inbuilt dual cameras.
                     Support various kinds of payment options, including bank card payments, Near Field Communication
                     (“NFC”) contactless and QR code payments.
一般特點：                以安卓作業系統操作，獲得業內最新的PCI PTS 5.x認証。外型小巧，方便手持，具有特大高清電觸摸
                     屏、內置雙攝像頭。支持各類型支付方式，包括銀行卡支付、NFC非觸式及二維碼支付。


           A930                           A920                      A77*                   A60                     A50*




ALL-IN-ONE SMART ELECTRONIC CASH REGISTER SOLUTION (E-SERIES)
智能管理及支付一體化解決方案（E系列）

General Features:    Powered by Android operating system, the all-in-one integrated solution delivers cost efficient and
                     space saving features to merchants. Certified with PCI PTS 5.x, the latest certification in the industry.
                     Stylish, large color touch screen, customer-facing display and an inbuilt camera. Support various kinds of
                     payment options including bank card payments, NFC contactless and QR code payments.
一般特點：                以安卓作業系統操作的一體化解決方案，使商戶更節省空間和成本。產品擁有業內最新PCI PTS 5.x認
                     証。外型時尚，擁有特大彩色觸摸屏、面向顧客的顯示屏、內置攝像頭，支持銀行卡支付，NFC非觸式
                     及二維碼等各類型支付方式。

Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（ 僅供參考）


ANDROID SMART TABLET SOLUTION
安卓智能平板解決方案

General features:    Android tablet-based solution is designed for retail and hospitality industry. PCI PTS 5.x certified and
                     includes many advanced features such as HD in-plane switching display and touch screen, front facing
                     camera, QR reader, innovative contactless reader and biometric fingerprint reader.
一般特點：                安卓智能平板解決方案專為零售及服務行業而設，具備PCI PTS 5.x認証，高清橫向顯示和觸控屏、前
                     置攝像頭、二維碼讀取器，新穎的非接觸式讀取器和生物指紋讀取器。


                                                AR6*                                            AR8*




IM SERIES UNATTENDED SOLUTION
IM系列自助服務解決方案

General features:    Ideal for self-service sales points such as petrol stations, parking, kiosk. PCI PTS 5.x certified and has met
                     required standards for anti-vandalism and waterproofing. Various payment methods are accepted and
                     connectivity options include 3G, 4G, WiFi and Bluetooth.
一般特點：                適用於加油站、停車場、售貨亭等多個應用場景。通過PCI PTS 5.x認證，符合防破壞和防水標準，可支
                     援各類型支付方式及3G、4G、WiFi和藍牙多個連接選項。

PAXSTORE

Features:             A secure cloud-based Software-as-a-Service (“SaaS”) platform that allows you to create and manage
                      your own independent marketplace for software application distribution. It also provides an integrated
                      terminal management system platform and smart data analysis.
特點：                   一個安全的雲端軟體即服務(「SaaS 」)平台，允許您創建及管理您自已獨立的軟件應用市場。同時，它
                      提供一個終端綜合管理系統平台和智能數據分析。

Directors and Senior Management
董事及高級管理層

and strengthen the preventive and control measures in all offices, in        疫情防控工作。疫情無疑會為本集團的營運及財
response to different global conditions. Undoubtedly, the outbreak           務表現帶來短暫影響，但在全球客戶、業務夥伴、
will pose a short-term impact on the Group’s operation and financial         供應商及員工的共同努力下，我們有信心不會對
performance. However, together with the joint efforts of our customers,      業務帶來重大的長遠影響。
business partners, suppliers and employees worldwide, we are confident

Non-GAAP measures                                                                   非公認會計原則指標

Certain non-GAAP (generally accepted accounting principles) measures, such as       若干非公認會計原則指標，例如主要財務資料（ 不包
key financial information excluding share-based payment expenses, are used for      括 股 份 支 付 費 用 ），乃 用 於 評 估 本 集 團 的 表 現。但 該
assessing the Group’s performance. These non-GAAP measures are not expressly        等非公認會計原則指標並非香港公認會計原則所明
permitted measures under GAAP in Hong Kong and may not be comparable                確認可的指標，故未必可與其他公司的同類指標作比
to similarly titled measures for other companies. Accordingly, such non-GAAP        較，因此，該等非公認會計原則指標不應視作經營收
measures should not be considered as an alternative to operating income as an       入（ 作為本集團業務表現指標 ）的替補或經營活動現
indicator of the operating performance of the Group or as an alternative to cash    金 流 量（ 作 為 衡 量 流 動 資 金 ）的 替 補。提 供 非 公 認 會
flows from operating activities as a measure of liquidity. The use of non-GAAP      計原則指標純綷為加強對本集團現時財務表現的整
measures is provided solely to enhance the overall understanding of the Group       體理解，此外由於本集團以往曾向投資者報告若干採
current financial performance. Additionally because the Group has historically      用非公認會計原則計算的業績，因此本集團認為包括
reported certain non-GAAP results to investors, the Group considers the inclusion   非公認會計原則指標可為本集團的財務報表提供一
of non-GAAP measures provides consistency in our financial reporting.               致 性。

Risk Management and Internal Control Systems                                 風險管理及內部監控系統
The risk management and internal control systems of the Company              本公司的風險管理及內部監控系統旨在管理而
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to          非 消 除 未 能 達 成 業 務 目 標 的 風 險，而 且 只 能 就
achieve business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not         所 有 風 險 問 題 作 出 合 理 但 非 絕 對 的 保 證。我 們
absolute assurance against all risk issues. We have employed a bottom-       已 採 取 由 下 而 上 的 方 法 以 識 別、評 估 及 減 低 在
up approach for identification, assessment and mitigation of risk at all     所有業務層面及各功能範疇上的風險。
business unit levels and across all functional areas.

Process used to identify, evaluate and manage significant                    用以識別、評估及管理重大風險的程序
risks
During the process of risk assessment, the Audit Committee identifies         在 風 險 評 估 過 程 中，審 核 委 員 會 識 別 本 集 團 的
the risks of the Group and decides on the respective risk levels and the      風 險 並 決 定 有 關 的 風 險 水 平，而 董 事 會 則 會 評
Board shall assess and determine the nature and extent of the risks that      估及釐定本集團於實現其策略目標時可接受的
are acceptable to the Group when achieving its strategic objectives.          風 險 性 質 和 程 度。考 慮 到 有 關 的 風 險 應 對 措 施
Taking into consideration the risk response, i.e. the necessary risk         （ 即 必 須 採 取 的 風 險 紓 緩 措 施）後，相 關 部 門 和
mitigating measures, the relevant departments and business units shall        業務單位將根據其職能及職責獲指派執行相應
be assigned to implement the risk management solutions in accordance          的 風 險 管 理 解 決 方 案。已 識 別 之 風 險 連 同 有 關

ENVIRONMENTAL POLICIES AND PERFORMANCE                                     環境政策及表現
The Group values the importance of protecting the environment in           本集團在營運過程中重視環境保護的重要性。
the process of operation. As the Group’s business does not involve         由 於 本 集 團 的 業 務 不 涉 獵 生 產 線，有 關 的 廢 棄
production lines, major wastes comprise terminal machines and tools        物主要包括客戶使用後需要報廢的終端機具。
to be disposed of post customer consumption. To mitigate the adverse       回收的報廢機具會交由政府認可的回收公司進
impact on the environment, waste machines and tools are transferred to     行電子垃圾回收和分解，以減低對環境的影響。
government-recognised recycling companies for undergoing electronic        我們近年亦加強環保辦公的理念包括提倡無紙
waste recycling and decomposition. In recent years, we have also           化、使用節能電器如LED光管及感應水龍頭、廢
strengthened the green office concept by promoting paperless office,       物分類及回收和鼓勵使用通過視頻形式召開會
use of energy-saving electrical appliances such as LED light pipes and     議取代出差以減少能源使用及碳足印。
inductive faucets, wastes classification and recycling, and encouraging
video conferencing in place of physical business trips to reduce energy
consumption and carbon footprint.

1    GENERAL INFORMATION                                                    1   一般資料
     PAX Global Technology Limited (the “Company”) is an investment             百 富 環 球 科 技 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 一
     holding company and together with its subsidiaries (collectively           間 投 資 控 股 公 司，連 同 其 附 屬 公 司（ 統 稱
     referred to as the “Group”) are principally engaged in the                 「本集團」）主要從事開發及銷售電子支付
     development and sales of electronic funds transfer point-of-               銷售點（「電子支付終端」）產品、提供維護
     sale (“E-payment Terminals”) products, provision of maintenance            及 安 裝 服 務（ 統 稱「電 子 支 付 終 端 解 決 方
     and installation services (collectively, the “E-payment Terminals          案業務」）。
     solutions business”).

     The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in                 本公司為一間在百慕達註冊成立的有
     Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Clarendon House,          限 責 任 公 司。本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 的 地 址
     2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda.                                  為 Clarendon House, 2 Church Street,
                                                                                Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda。

     The Company’s shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock            本公司股份於二零一零年十二月二十日在
     Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) on 20                 香 港 聯 合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司（「聯 交 所」）主
     December 2010.                                                             板上市。

         (i)    Subsidiaries                                                     (i)    附屬公司
                Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Group                      附屬公司指本集團對其有控制
                has control. The Group controls an entity when                          權 的 所 有 實 體。本 集 團 對 實 體
                the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable                     有 控 制 權，是 指 本 集 團 因 參 與
                returns from its involvement with the entity and has                    該實體的營運而獲得或有權享
                the ability to affect those returns through its power                   有 其 可 變 回 報，並 能 夠 運 用 其
                to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are                指導實體活動的權力影響上述
                fully consolidated from the date on which control is                    回 報。附 屬 公 司 在 控 制 權 轉 移
                transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated                       至 本 集 團 當 日 悉 數 綜 合 入 賬。
                from the date that control ceases.                                      附屬公司在控制權終止之日起
                                                                                        停止綜合入賬。

                The acquisition method of accounting is used to                         本集團的業務合併使用收購會
                account for business combinations by the Group.                         計法入賬。

                gains on transactions between group companies are                       團公司間交易的未變現收益均
                eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless                予 以 抵 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會 對
                the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                      銷，除 非 該 交 易 有 證 據 顯 示 所
                of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                        轉 讓 資 產 出 現 減 值 則 作 別 論。
                subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                       附屬公司的會計政策已按需要

                Group.                                                                  政策貫徹一致。

    2.3 Business combinations                                                2.3 業務合併
        The acquisition method of accounting is used to account                  所有業務合併均以收購會計法入賬，
        for all business combinations, regardless of whether equity              無論所收購者為權益工具或其他資
        instruments or other assets are acquired. The consideration              產。收購一間附屬公司所轉讓的代價
        transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:           包括：

        •    fair values of the assets transferred                               •   所轉讓資產的公平值

        •    liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired           •   所收購對象先前擁有人產生的
             business                                                                負債

        •    equity interests issued by the Group                                •   本集團發行的股權

        •    fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a               •   或然代價安排產生的任何資產
             contingent consideration arrangement, and                               或負債的公平值；及

        •    fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the               •   任何先前存在的附屬公司股權
             subsidiary.                                                             的公平值

        Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent              除有限例外情況外，於業務合併收購
        liabilities assumed in a business combination are, with                  的可識別資產以及承擔的負債及或
        limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair values              然負債，初步按收購日期的公平值計
        at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-                   量。本集團根據個別收購交易按公平
        controlling interest in the acquired entity on an acquisition-           值或非控股權益應佔被收購實體可
        by-acquisition basis either at fair value or at the non-                 識別資產淨值的比例確認於被收購
        controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired               實體的任何非控股權益。
        entity’s net identifiable assets.

        Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                      收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

        The excess of the

        •    consideration transferred,                                          •   所轉讓代價，

        •    amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired              •   於被收購實體的任何非控股權
             entity, and                                                             益金額，及

        •    acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity                  •   任何先前於被收購實體的權益
             interest in the acquired entity                                         於收購日期的公平值

        over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is           與已收購可識別資產淨值的公平值
        recorded as goodwill. If those amounts are less than the fair            的差額按商譽列賬。倘上述金額低於
        value of the net identifiable assets of the business acquired,           所收購業務可識別資產淨值的公平
        the difference is recognised directly in the consolidated                值，有關差額會作為一項廉價購買直

              Regular way purchases and sales of financial assets                    以日常方式買賣的金融資產

              Group commits to purchase or sell the asset. Financial                 團 承 諾 購 買 或 出 售 資 產 之 日。
              assets are derecognised when the rights to receive                     當金融資產收取現金流量的權

         Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount           倘若存在合法可強制執行的權利以
         is reported in the consolidated balance sheet when there is              抵銷所確認金額及擬按淨額基準結

         and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise            融資產與負債相互抵銷，而淨額於綜
         the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The legally           合資產負債表內呈報。法定可執行權
         enforceable right must not be contingent on future events                利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在
         and must be enforceable in the normal course of business                 一般業務過程中以及倘本公司或對
         and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the             手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或破
         Company or the counterparty.                                             產時，這也必須具有約束力。

      2.23 Provisions                                                         2.23 撥備
          Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                 當本集團因過往事件承擔現有法律
          legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events;           或推定責任，而解除責任很有可能導
          it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required           致資源流出，且金額能夠可靠計算的
          to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably             情況下，便會確認撥備。
          estimated.

          Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                   倘承擔若干類似責任，於釐定解除責
          likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement              任是否導致資源流出時，將以整類責
          is determined by considering the class of obligations as a             任類別為考慮。即使同類責任當中任
          whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an          何一項導致資源流出可能性甚低，亦
          outflow with respect to any one item included in the same              會確認撥備。
          class of obligations may be small.

          Provisions are measured at the present value of the                    撥備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算
          expenditures expected to be required to settle the obligation          有關責任的支出現值計量，該利率反
          using a pre-tax rate that reflects current market assessments          映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關責
          of the time value of money and the risks specific to the               任固有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而
          obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of            增加的撥備確認為利息開支。
          time is recognised as interest expense.

      2.26 Leases (as the lessee for operating leases)                            2.26 租約（經營租約的承租人）
          As explained in note 2.1.1(a), the Group has changed its                  如 附 註2.1.1(a)所 述，本 集 團 已 更 改
          accounting policy for leases where the Group is the lessee.               其作為承租人的租賃的會計政策。新
          The new policy is described below and the impact of the                   政策於下文闡述及該更改的影響載
          change in note 2.1.1(a).                                                  於附註2.1.1(a)。

          Until 31 December 2018, leases in which a significant                     直至二零一八年十二月三十一日，凡
          portion of the risks and rewards of ownership were not                    擁有權所涉及大部分風險及回報未
          transferred to the Group as lessee were classified as                     轉移至作出承租人的本集團的租約，
          operating leases. Payments made under operating leases                    均列作經營租約。根據經營租約所付
          (net of any incentives received from the lessor) were charged             租金在扣除來自出租人的任何優惠
          to profit or loss on a straight-line basis over the period of the         後，在租約年期內以直線法於損益中
          lease.                                                                    支銷。

          From 1 January 2019, leases are recognised as a right-of-use              自二零一九年一月一日起，於租賃資
          asset and a corresponding liability at the date at which the              產可供本集團使用的日期，就租賃確
          leased asset is available for use by the Group.                           認為資產使用權及相應負債。

          Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially                 租賃產生的資產及負債初步以現值
          measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include              進行計量。租賃負債包括固定租賃付
          the net present value of the fixed lease payments. Lease                  款的淨現值。根據合理確定延續選擇
          payments to be made under reasonably certain extension                    權支付的租賃付款亦計入負債計量
          options are also included in the measurement of the liability.            之內。

          The lease payments are discounted using the interest                      租賃付款採用租賃所隱含的利率予
          rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily                以貼現。倘無法輕易確定該利率（ 為
          determined, which is generally the case for leases in the                 本集團租賃的一般情況），則使用承
          Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                   租人的增量借款利率，即個別承租人
          being the rate that the individual lessee would have to                   在類似經濟環境中按類似條款、抵押
          pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                   及條件借入獲得與使用權資產具有
          similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic             類似價值的資產所需資金而必須支
          environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                  付的利率。為釐定增量借款利率，本
          To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group,                   集團在可能情況下，使用個別承租人
          where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by             最近獲得的第三方融資為出發點作
          the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect            出調整，以反映自獲得第三方融資以
          changes in financing conditions since third party financing               來融資條件的變動，及進行特定於租
          was received and makes any adjustments specific to the                    約 的 調 整，例 如 期 限、國 家、貨 幣 及
          lease, e.g. term, country, currency and security.                         抵押。

          Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance                租賃付款於本金及財務費用之間作
          cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the              出分配。財務費用在租賃期間於損益
          lease period so as to produce a constant periodic rate of                 扣除，藉以令各期間的負債餘額的期
          interest on the remaining balance of the liability for each               間利率一致。
          period.

         of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on a straight-             或租賃期（ 以較短者為準）按直線法

         the underlying asset’s useful life.                                      的可使用年期內予以折舊。

         line basis as an expense in profit or loss. Short-term leases            確認為開支。短期租賃為租賃期12個
         are leases with a lease term of 12 months or less.                       月或以下的租賃。

         reasonable assurance that the refund will be received which              團乃於綜合收益表的「其他收入」內

         will be received and the Group will comply with all the                  表「其他收入」確認。
         attached conditions.

         and costs can be measured reliably. Other development                    發展開支於產生時支銷。過往確認為
         expenditures are expensed as incurred. Development costs                 開支的發展成本不會於往後期間確

    3.2 Capital management                                                     3.2 資本管理
        The Group regards its shareholders’ equity as capital. The                 本集團將其股東權益視為資本。本集
        Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                  團管理資本的目的為保障本集團繼
        the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order                續以持續經營方式為股東提供回報
        to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other                 以及為其他利益相關者帶來利益，並
        stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to               且維持最佳資本結構以減少資金成
        reduce the cost of capital.                                                本。

        In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the                  為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可調
        Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to                           整向股東支付股息的金額、將資本退
        shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue new                    還予股東、發行新股份或出售資產以
        shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                      減少債項。

        The Group monitors capital on the basis of gearing ratio.                  本集團基於資本負債比率監控資本。
        This ratio is calculated as total debt divided by total capital.           該比率按債項總額除以資本總額計
        Total debt includes borrowings of the Group. Total capital is              算。債項總額包括本集團借貸。資本
        calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated balance                總 額 為 列 於 綜 合 資 產 負 債 表 的「權
        sheet plus total debt.                                                     益」加上債項總額計算。

        As at 31 December 2019 and 2018, the gearing ratio was as                  於二零一九年及二零一八年十二月
        follows:                                                                   三十一日，資本負債比率為如下：

      3.3 Fair value estimation                                                        3.3 公平值估計
          (i)   Fair value hierarchy                                                          (i)     公平值層級
                The table below analyses the Group’s financial                                        下表分析本集團於二零一九年
                instruments carried at fair values as at 31 December                                  及二零一八年十二月三十一日
                2019 and 2018 by level of valuation techniques used                                   以計量公平值所用的估值技術
                to measure fair values. Such inputs are categorised into                              層級劃分的按公平值入賬金融
                three levels within a fair value hierarchy as follows:                                工 具。有 關 輸 入 數 據 分 類 為 公
                                                                                                      平值層級內的三個層級如下：

                •      Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for                               •        相同資產或負債的活躍市
                       identical assets or liabilities (level 1).                                              場 報 價（ 未 經 調 整 ）（ 第
                                                                                                               一級）。

                •      Inputs other than quoted prices included                                       •     除包含於第一級的報價
                       within level 1 that are observable for the asset                                     外，資 產 或 負 債 的 可 觀
                       or liability, either directly (that is, as prices) or                                察 直 接（ 即 價 格 ）或 間 接
                       indirectly (that is, derived from prices) (level 2).                                （ 即 源 自 價 格 者 ）輸 入 資
                                                                                                            料（ 第二級）。

4   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                        4   重要會計估計及判斷
    JUDGEMENTS
    Estimates and judgements used in preparing the consolidated                  用於編製綜合財務報表的估計及判斷，乃
    financial statements are evaluated based on historical experience            基於過往經驗及其他因素，包括預期日後
    and other factors, including expectations of future events that are          在有關情況下相信會合理出現的事件而作
    believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.                           出。

    The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the                     本集團作出有關未來的估計及假設。按此
    future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,              規範，所作的會計估計甚少與有關的實際
    seldom equal to the related actual results. The estimates and                結果相同。下文載列有極大風險會導致須
    assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material               對下一個財政年度資產與負債的賬面值作
    adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within          出重大調整的估計及假設。
    the next financial year are addressed below.

    (a)   Income taxes and deferred taxation                                     (a)   所得稅及遞延稅款
          Significant judgement is required in determining the                         對所得稅釐定撥備時，需要作出重要
          provision for income tax. There are many transactions                        的判斷。許多交易及計算的最終釐定
          and calculations for which the ultimate determination is                     無法確定。當最終的稅款結果與最初
          uncertain. Where the final tax outcome of these matters is                   記賬金額不同時，有關差額將影響釐
          different from the amounts that were initially recorded, such                定期間的當期稅項和遞延稅款資產
          difference will impact the current tax and deferred tax assets               及負債。
          and liabilities in the period in which such determination is
          made.

          Deferred tax assets relating to certain temporary differences                當管理層認為將來很有可能有應課
          and tax losses are recognised when management considers it                   稅溢利以扺銷暫時性差額或可使用
          is probable that future taxable profit will be available against             稅務虧損時，有關若干暫時性差額及
          which the temporary differences or tax losses can be utilised.               稅務虧損的遞延稅項資產予以確認。
          The outcome of their actual utilisation may be different.                    實際使用的結果可能不同。

 Notes to the Consolidated Financial Statements
 綜合財務報表附註


 4    CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                        4   重要會計估計及判斷（續）
      JUDGEMENTS (continued)

      (b) Provision for inventories                                                (b)   存貨撥備
             Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable             存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值兩者之
             value. The cost of inventories is written down to net                       間較低者入賬。倘有客觀證據顯示存
             realisable value when there is an objective evidence that                   貨成本或不能收回，則將存貨成本撇
